# National counterparts of the generic medium-tank bookmark designs.
# Names follow the existing legacy equipment ladder. See National_Tank_Presets.md.
# Called before OOB loading, and after major-producer 1980 research.

cwic_create_national_tank_variants = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "No Step Back"
			tag = USA
			has_tech = nsb_iw_armored_vehicles
			NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_starting_medium_tank_chassis_0_created }
		}
		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "M4 Sherman"
			type = medium_tank_chassis_0
			allow_without_tech = yes

				main_armament_slot = tank_medium_cannon1

# APC/IFV national helpers are interleaved with generic fallbacks by ascending hull.
# Historical names use baseline recipes; see APC_IFV_Preset_Manifest.json.

cwic_create_national_apc_chassis_0_variants = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "No Step Back"
			tag = ADR
			has_tech = nsb_apc_hulls0
			NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_starting_apc_chassis_0_created }
		}
		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "Sd.Kfz. 251/1 W/ IR"
			type = apc_chassis_0
			allow_without_tech = yes
			parent_version = 0
			mark_older_equipment_obsolete = yes
			upgrades = { tank_engine_upgrade = 0 tank_armor_upgrade = 0 }
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = apc_firing_ports

				tank_special_slot_4 = empty
				tank_special_slot_5 = empty
				tank_special_slot_6 = empty
				tank_special_slot_7 = empty
